@@ 1398 -0 +39
+  /**
+   * A NEW BRANCH WITH NO HISTORY (prod, 2026-11-13: "git checkout --orphan codeboss-plugins" was refused, and the agent
+   * wrote .git/HEAD by hand). HEAD names a branch that has no commit yet. checkout --orphan keeps the index and the work
+   * tree (from <start> when one is given); switch --orphan (empty) takes every tracked file out of both - refused, as git
+   * does, while a tracked file has changes of its own - and leaves untracked files where they are.
+   */
+  static int orphanBranch(String name, String start, boolean empty, boolean quiet) throws Exception {
+    if (repo.findRef(Constants.R_HEADS + name) != null) throw fatal("a branch named '" + name + "' already exists");
+    if (!Repository.isValidRefName(Constants.R_HEADS + name)) throw fatal("'" + name + "' is not a valid branch name");
+    if (start != null) {
+      ObjectId id = need(start);
+      try { git.checkout().setName(id.name()).call(); }
+      catch (CheckoutConflictException e) { return conflictsOnSwitch(e.getConflictingPaths(), "checkout"); }
+    }
+    if (empty) {
+      org.eclipse.jgit.api.Status st = git.status().call();
+      Set<String> changed = new TreeSet<String>();
+      changed.addAll(st.getModified()); changed.addAll(st.getChanged()); changed.addAll(st.getAdded()); changed.addAll(st.getRemoved()); changed.addAll(st.getMissing());
+      if (!changed.isEmpty()) return conflictsOnSwitch(new ArrayList<String>(changed), "checkout");
+      DirCache dc = repo.lockDirCache();
+      try {
+        File top = repo.getWorkTree();
+        for (int i = 0; i < dc.getEntryCount(); i++) {
+          File f = new File(top, dc.getEntry(i).getPathString());
+          if (f.isFile() && !f.delete()) throw fatal("could not remove '" + dc.getEntry(i).getPathString() + "'");
+          for (File d = f.getParentFile(); d != null && !d.equals(top); d = d.getParentFile()) { String[] in = d.list(); if (in == null || in.length > 0 || !d.delete()) break; }
+        }
+        dc.clear();
+        dc.write();
+        dc.commit();
+      } finally { dc.unlock(); }
+    }
+    RefUpdate u = repo.updateRef(Constants.HEAD);
+    RefUpdate.Result r = u.link(Constants.R_HEADS + name);
+    if (r != RefUpdate.Result.NEW && r != RefUpdate.Result.FORCED && r != RefUpdate.Result.NO_CHANGE) throw fatal("could not point HEAD at '" + name + "' (" + r + ")");
+    if (!quiet) err.println("Switched to a new branch '" + name + "'");
+    return 0;
+  }
+

+// WHAT TO COUNT UNPUSHED COMMITS AGAINST, read from the repo's files - no git call (a scan of 15 repos went from 2.5 s to
+// 5 s when every repo asked git twice more, and in the container each call is a JGit call of 0.5-1 s): ['@{u}', then
+// 'origin/<branch>'] when the branch has a section in .git/config, ['origin/<branch>'] when only that remote ref is on
+// disk, [] when there is nothing to count against. A .git that is a FILE (a worktree, a submodule) is not read here:
+// both are asked of git, as before.
+function gpUnpushedRefs(repoDir, branch) {
+  if (!branch || /^\(|^HEAD$/.test(branch)) return [];
+  const gitDir = path.join(repoDir, '.git');
+  let isDir = false; try { isDir = statSync(gitDir).isDirectory(); } catch { return []; }
+  if (!isDir) return ['@{u}', `origin/${branch}`];
+  const read = (f) => { try { return readFileSync(path.join(gitDir, f), 'utf8'); } catch { return ''; } };
+  const lines = (f) => read(f).split('\n').map((l) => l.trim());
+  const refs = [];
+  if (lines('config').includes(`[branch "${branch}"]`)) refs.push('@{u}');
+  if (existsSync(path.join(gitDir, 'refs', 'remotes', 'origin', ...branch.split('/'))) || lines('packed-refs').some((l) => l.endsWith(` refs/remotes/origin/${branch}`))) refs.push(`origin/${branch}`);
+  return refs;
+}
+// (one count per ref tried, the first that answers: the branch's upstream, else origin/<branch> - as the push window reads it)
+function gpUnpushed(run, repoDir, branch) {
+  for (const ref of gpUnpushedRefs(repoDir, branch)) {
+    const n = run(['rev-list', '--count', `${ref}..HEAD`]);
+    const v = n == null ? NaN : parseInt(n.trim(), 10);
+    if (Number.isFinite(v)) return v;
+  }
+  return null;

+/**
+ * THE AGENT'S BROWSER IN CODE_BOSS'S OWN WINDOW, from Node (a port of the manager's PanelBroker.java, so the same
+ * broker works when code_boss runs outside the container - and, later, when the manager spawns this one). With
+ * Settings > Agent's browser on, a plugin that asks for a browser is given THIS instead of a Chrome of its own: an
+ * address that speaks DevTools like a whole browser, but whose one page is the iframe in the right panel of
+ * code_boss's window - so the developer watches every click the agent makes.
+ *
+ * HOW: code_boss's window is opened with a debugging port while the setting is on - a port only this broker connects
+ * to. The page in the panel is on another SITE than the window (127.0.0.1, the window is codeboss.localhost), so
+ * Chrome gives the iframe a process and a DevTools target of its own (the target's id is the iframe's frame id, the
+ * same for its whole life - measured, test/_panel-oopif-spike.mjs). A plugin's connection here gets a connection of
+ * its own to that Chrome, and:
+ *   - the browser-level calls are ANSWERED HERE: Target.getTargets lists one page (the panel), createTarget navigates
+ *     the panel, attachToTarget attaches to the iframe's target, closeTarget blanks the panel;
+ *   - every call on the page's session goes to the iframe's target, as it is, except: Page.navigate keeps the page off
+ *     the window's own site (codeboss.localhost -> 127.0.0.1: on the window's site the iframe would join the window's
+ *     process and its target would end); Page.handleJavaScriptDialog goes to the window's session (a dialog in the
+ *     iframe is announced there, measured); Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride sizes the iframe (Chrome refuses it on
+ *     an iframe);
+ *   - NOTHING ELSE of the window is reachable: no other target, no Browser.*, Target.*, Storage.*, SystemInfo.* -
+ *     refused with a sentence. (On its own session, Chrome would list every target of the browser - measured.)
+ *   - a page that refuses to be framed (X-Frame-Options, CSP frame-ancestors) is shown anyway: this broker's own
+ *     session on the iframe takes those two headers off its documents (Fetch.fulfillRequest - continueResponse does
+ *     not take; measured, test/_panel-xfo-spike.mjs). A plugin's own Fetch.enable works beside it.
+ *
+ * WHO MAY CONNECT: it listens on 127.0.0.1 only. A request with an Origin header is a web page, and is refused -
+ * Chrome's own DevTools does the same. The one thing a page may load from here is /blank (the empty page the panel
+ * starts on).
+ *
+ * WHAT IS NOT HERE (the container's parts of the Java): the bridge into the container (Mux, insidePort), the
+ * container's and the instance's state checks, and the instance itself - the caller says where the window's profile
+ * is, what its site is, and how to tell code_boss's page what the panel shows (tellPage).
+ *
+ * Zero dependencies: both ends of the WebSocket are written here (RFC 6455, text messages only - DevTools sends
+ * nothing else), as the Java does.
+ */
+import net from 'node:net';
+import http from 'node:http';
+import { randomBytes, randomUUID, createHash } from 'node:crypto';
+import { readFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';

+const GUID = '258EAFA5-E914-47DA-95CA-C5AB0DC85B11';
+/** On the page's session these whole domains reach past the page (the browser, its other targets, every site's storage). */
+const DENY = new Set(['Target', 'Browser', 'SystemInfo', 'Tethering', 'Storage', 'Tracing', 'BackgroundService', 'Extensions', 'PWA', 'Cast', 'DeviceAccess', 'FedCm', 'Autofill']);
+/** Calls whose effect a session keeps: replayed when the page's target has to be attached again. */
+const STICKY = new Set(['Page.setLifecycleEventsEnabled', 'Page.addScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument', 'Network.setCacheDisabled', 'Network.emulateNetworkConditions', 'Network.setExtraHTTPHeaders', 'Network.setUserAgentOverride', 'Network.setBlockedURLs', 'Runtime.setAsyncCallStackDepth', 'Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate', 'Emulation.setEmulatedMedia', 'Emulation.setTimezoneOverride', 'Emulation.setGeolocationOverride', 'Emulation.setLocaleOverride', 'Emulation.setUserAgentOverride']);
+const MAX_MESSAGE = 512 * 1024 * 1024;
+const MAX_HEAD = 16384;
+const NOTE = "This browser is the right panel of code_boss's window (Settings > Agent's browser): one page, which the developer watches. Target.createTarget navigates it, closeTarget blanks it; no other tab or window can be opened, and the window itself is not reachable.";
+
+const hex = (bytes) => randomBytes(bytes).toString('hex').toUpperCase();
+const pbSleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+// the Java's Json helpers: what is not there reads as empty, never as an exception
+const pbStr = (v) => (v == null ? '' : String(v));
+const int = (v) => (typeof v === 'number' ? Math.round(v) : 0);
+const objOf = (v) => (v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) ? v : {});
+const arr = (v) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []);
+const no = (why) => ({ ok: false, error: why });
+const escapeRe = (s) => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');

+/**
+ * THE WINDOW HAS A SITE OF ITS OWN (codeboss.localhost - Chrome resolves every *.localhost to this machine, and it is
+ * another site than localhost), so an app is shown at ITS OWN address: localhost stays localhost, and the frames it
+ * loads at its own address stay same-origin with it - searchable from the page, as in any Chrome (measured,
+ * test/_panel-nested-spike.mjs: rewriting the app to 127.0.0.1 made its localhost frames cross-origin). Only an
+ * address on the window's own site is moved (to 127.0.0.1): there the iframe would join the window's process.
+ */
+export function panelSiteOf(url, windowHost = 'codeboss.localhost') {
+  if (url == null) return '';
+  return String(url).replace(new RegExp('^(https?://)' + escapeRe(windowHost) + '(?=[:/?#]|$)', 'i'), (_, scheme) => scheme + '127.0.0.1');

+// ── a WebSocket, either end (RFC 6455; text messages only - DevTools sends nothing else) ──
+
+class Ws {
+  /** masks: a client masks what it sends; a server never does. rest: what came after the HTTP head. */
+  constructor(socket, masks, rest) {
+    this.s = socket;
+    this.masks = masks;
+    this.closed = false;
+    this.chunks = [];
+    this.have = 0;
+    this.parts = [];
+    this.queue = [];   // messages that came before anyone listened (a plugin may speak before Chrome is connected)
+    this.onText = null;
+    this.onEnd = null;
+    socket.setNoDelay(true);
+    socket.on('data', (d) => this.feed(d));
+    socket.on('close', () => this.end());
+    socket.on('error', () => {});   // ('close' follows)
+    if (rest && rest.length) this.feed(rest);
+    socket.resume();
+  }
+
+  /** Every text message from here on goes to onText; onEnd once, when the other end closed. */
+  listen(onText, onEnd) {
+    this.onText = onText;
+    this.onEnd = onEnd;
+    const q = this.queue;
+    this.queue = [];
+    for (const t of q) onText(t);
+    if (this.closed) onEnd();
+  }
+
+  /** false: the connection is gone (the Java's IOException). */
+  send(text) { return this.frame(0x1, Buffer.from(text, 'utf8')); }
+
+  frame(op, p) {
+    if (this.closed) return false;
+    const m = this.masks ? 0x80 : 0;
+    let h;
+    if (p.length < 126) { h = Buffer.alloc(2); h[1] = m | p.length; }
+    else if (p.length < 65536) { h = Buffer.alloc(4); h[1] = m | 126; h.writeUInt16BE(p.length, 2); }
+    else { h = Buffer.alloc(10); h[1] = m | 127; h.writeBigUInt64BE(BigInt(p.length), 2); }
+    h[0] = 0x80 | op;
+    let body = p;
+    let key = null;
+    if (this.masks) {
+      key = randomBytes(4);
+      body = Buffer.allocUnsafe(p.length);
+      for (let k = 0; k < p.length; k++) body[k] = p[k] ^ key[k & 3];
+    }
+    try { this.s.write(key ? Buffer.concat([h, key, body]) : Buffer.concat([h, body])); return true; }
+    catch { this.end(); return false; }
+  }
+
+  /** The first n bytes, in one buffer (the chunks are joined only when the first one is too short). */
+  peek(n) {
+    if (this.chunks[0].length >= n) return this.chunks[0];
+    const b = Buffer.concat(this.chunks);
+    this.chunks = [b];
+    return b;
+  }
+
+  take(n) {
+    const b = this.chunks.length === 1 ? this.chunks[0] : Buffer.concat(this.chunks);
+    this.chunks = b.length > n ? [b.subarray(n)] : [];
+    this.have -= n;
+    return b.subarray(0, n);
+  }
+
+  feed(d) {
+    if (this.closed) return;
+    this.chunks.push(d);
+    this.have += d.length;
+    while (this.have >= 2) {
+      const buf = this.peek(Math.min(this.have, 14));
+      const b0 = buf[0], b1 = buf[1];
+      let len = b1 & 0x7f, off = 2;
+      if (len === 126) { if (this.have < 4) return; len = buf.readUInt16BE(2); off = 4; }
+      else if (len === 127) {
+        if (this.have < 10) return;
+        const big = buf.readBigUInt64BE(2);
+        if (big > BigInt(MAX_MESSAGE)) { this.end(); return; }   // a WebSocket message over 512 MB
+        len = Number(big);
+        off = 10;
+      }
+      if (len > MAX_MESSAGE) { this.end(); return; }
+      const masked = (b1 & 0x80) !== 0;
+      const start = off + (masked ? 4 : 0);
+      if (this.have < start + len) return;   // (a big message waits whole, its chunks joined once)
+      const all = this.take(start + len);
+      const p = all.subarray(start);
+      if (masked) { const key = all.subarray(off, off + 4); for (let k = 0; k < p.length; k++) p[k] ^= key[k & 3]; }
+      const op = b0 & 0x0f;
+      if (op === 0x8) { this.frame(0x8, Buffer.alloc(0)); this.end(); return; }
+      if (op === 0x9) { this.frame(0xA, Buffer.from(p)); continue; }
+      if (op === 0xA) continue;
+      this.parts.push(Buffer.from(p));
+      if ((b0 & 0x80) !== 0) {
+        const text = (this.parts.length === 1 ? this.parts[0] : Buffer.concat(this.parts)).toString('utf8');
+        this.parts = [];
+        if (this.onText) this.onText(text); else this.queue.push(text);
+        if (this.closed) return;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  end() {
+    if (this.closed) return;
+    this.closed = true;
+    try { this.s.destroy(); } catch { /* gone */ }
+    if (this.onEnd) this.onEnd();
+  }
+
+  close() { this.end(); }
+
+  /** A client connection to ws://127.0.0.1:port/path. */
+  static connect(port, p) {
+    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+      const s = net.connect({ host: '127.0.0.1', port });
+      s.on('error', () => {});
+      const t = setTimeout(() => { s.destroy(); reject(new Error('Chrome did not take the DevTools connection (no answer)')); }, 5000);
+      s.once('connect', async () => {
+        s.setNoDelay(true);
+        s.write('GET ' + p + ' HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:' + port + '\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Key: ' + randomBytes(16).toString('base64') + '\r\nSec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\n\r\n');
+        let r = null;
+        try { r = await readHead(s); } catch { /* r stays null */ }
+        clearTimeout(t);
+        if (!r || !r.head.startsWith('HTTP/1.1 101')) { s.destroy(); reject(new Error('Chrome did not take the DevTools connection (' + (r ? r.head.split('\r\n')[0] : 'no answer') + ')')); return; }
+        resolve(new Ws(s, true, r.rest));
+      });
+      s.once('close', () => { clearTimeout(t); reject(new Error('Chrome did not take the DevTools connection (no answer)')); });
+    });
+  }
+}
+
+/** An HTTP head, up to the blank line (16 KB at most), and what came after it. null: the connection closed first. */
+function readHead(s) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    let buf = Buffer.alloc(0), settled = false;
+    const done = (v, e) => {
+      if (settled) return;
+      settled = true;
+      s.removeListener('data', onData);
+      s.removeListener('end', onEnd);
+      s.removeListener('close', onEnd);
+      if (e) reject(e); else resolve(v);
+    };
+    const onData = (d) => {
+      buf = buf.length ? Buffer.concat([buf, d]) : d;
+      const i = buf.indexOf('\r\n\r\n');
+      if (i >= 0 && i + 4 <= MAX_HEAD) { s.pause(); done({ head: buf.subarray(0, i + 4).toString('latin1'), rest: buf.subarray(i + 4) }); return; }
+      if (buf.length >= MAX_HEAD) done(null, new Error('a request head over 16 KB'));
+    };
+    const onEnd = () => done(null);
+    s.on('data', onData);
+    s.once('end', onEnd);
+    s.once('close', onEnd);
+  });
+}
+
+function respond(s, code, type, body) {
+  const b = Buffer.from(body, 'utf8');
+  s.write('HTTP/1.1 ' + code + (code === 200 ? ' OK' : code === 404 ? ' Not Found' : code === 403 ? ' Forbidden' : ' Bad Request') + '\r\nContent-Type: ' + type + '\r\nContent-Length: ' + b.length + '\r\nCache-Control: no-store\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n');
+  s.end(b);
+  s.resume();   // (read the client's end, so the socket closes)
+}
+
+function httpGet(port, p) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const req = http.get({ host: '127.0.0.1', port, path: p, agent: false, timeout: 5000 }, (res) => {
+      const out = [];
+      res.on('data', (d) => out.push(d));
+      res.on('end', () => { if (res.statusCode >= 400) reject(new Error('HTTP ' + res.statusCode)); else resolve(Buffer.concat(out).toString('utf8')); });
+      res.on('error', reject);
+    });
+    req.on('timeout', () => req.destroy(new Error('no answer in 5 s')));
+    req.on('error', reject);
+  });
+}
+
+// ── the broker of one code_boss window ──
+
+class Broker {
+  constructor({ profileDir, windowOrigin, tellPage, downloadsDir, log }) {
+    this.profileDir = profileDir;
+    this.windowOrigin = String(windowOrigin || '').replace(/\/+$/, '');
+    let host = 'codeboss.localhost';
+    try { host = new URL(this.windowOrigin).hostname || host; } catch { /* the default site */ }
+    this.windowHost = host;
+    this.tell = tellPage;
+    this.downloadsDir = downloadsDir || '';
+    this.log = log;
+    this.id = 'panel';                    // the id open() answers with
+    this.targetId = hex(16);              // the panel's page, as a plugin sees it
+    this.browserPath = '/devtools/browser/' + randomUUID();
+    this.label = 'default';
+    this.url = 'about:blank';
+    this.title = '';
+    this.server = null;
+    this.port = 0;
+    this.stopped = false;
+    this.clients = new Set();
+    this.told = Promise.resolve();   // what was said to code_boss's page, in the order it was said
+  }
+
+  alive() { return !this.stopped && this.server != null && this.server.listening; }
+  blankUrl() { return 'http://127.0.0.1:' + this.port + '/blank'; }
+  shown(u) { return u != null && u.startsWith(this.blankUrl()) ? 'about:blank' : u; }
+  windowsOwn(u) { const w = this.windowOrigin; return u != null && (u.startsWith(w + '/') || u === w); }
+  siteOf(u) { return panelSiteOf(u, this.windowHost); }
+
+  async start() {
+    if (this.server) this.stop();
+    this.stopped = false;
+    // downloads land where a plugin reads them, as with a Chrome of its own (the Java's Browser.open makes the folder)
+    if (this.downloadsDir) { try { mkdirSync(this.downloadsDir, { recursive: true }); } catch { /* Chrome says so on the download */ } }
+    const server = net.createServer((s) => { this.serve(s); });
+    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+      server.once('error', reject);
+      server.listen({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: 0, backlog: 50 }, () => { server.removeListener('error', reject); resolve(); });
+    });
+    server.on('error', (e) => this.log('panel broker: ' + e.message));
+    this.server = server;
+    this.port = server.address().port;
+  }
+
+  stop() {
+    this.stopped = true;
+    try { if (this.server) this.server.close(); } catch { /* closed */ }
+    const cs = [...this.clients];
+    this.clients.clear();
+    for (const c of cs) c.close();
+  }
+
+  brief() {
+    return {
+      id: this.id,
+      label: this.label,
+      url: this.shown(this.url),
+      cdpUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:' + this.port,
+      wsUrl: 'ws://127.0.0.1:' + this.port + this.browserPath,
+      port: this.port,
+      downloads: this.downloadsDir,
+      panel: true,
+      note: NOTE,
+    };
+  }
+
+  /** Say to code_boss's page what the panel shows: url (null: keep it), show (open the panel on it), at (it is there already: do not load it). */
+  tellPage(u, show, at) {
+    if (u != null && !at) this.url = u;
+    // one after another, as the Java's (blocking) POSTs are: the page's address line ends on the last thing said
+    const next = this.told.then(async () => {
+      try { await this.tell(u, !!show, !!at); } catch (e) { this.log('panel broker: telling the page failed: ' + (e?.message || e)); }
+    });
+    this.told = next;
+    return next;
+  }
+
+  /** Chrome's DevTools port of the window, from its profile (it writes DevToolsActivePort when it opens it). */
+  async chromeEndpoint() {
+    // a window that was just opened (or opened again, when the setting changed) writes it within a second or two
+    const f = path.join(this.profileDir, 'DevToolsActivePort');
+    const until = Date.now() + 15000;
+    for (;;) {
+      try {
+        const lines = readFileSync(f, 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/);
+        if (lines.length >= 2 && /^\d{2,5}$/.test(lines[0].trim()) && lines[1].trim().startsWith('/devtools/')) return [lines[0].trim(), lines[1].trim()];
+      } catch { /* not yet */ }
+      if (Date.now() > until || this.stopped) throw new Error("code_boss's window has not opened its DevTools port (is the window open?)");
+      await pbSleep(150);
+    }
+  }
+
+  async serve(s) {
+    s.on('error', () => {});
+    try {
+      s.setNoDelay(true);
+      s.setTimeout(15000, () => s.destroy());
+      const r = await readHead(s);
+      if (!r) { s.destroy(); return; }
+      const lines = r.head.split('\r\n');
+      const rl = lines[0].split(' ');
+      const method = rl[0], p0 = rl.length > 1 ? rl[1] : '/';
+      const h = {};
+      for (let k = 1; k < lines.length; k++) { const c = lines[k].indexOf(':'); if (c > 0) h[lines[k].slice(0, c).trim().toLowerCase()] = lines[k].slice(c + 1).trim(); }
+      const host = h.host != null ? h.host : '127.0.0.1:' + this.port;
+      const hostName = host.startsWith('[') ? host.slice(0, host.indexOf(']') + 1) : host.split(':')[0];
+      const local = hostName === '127.0.0.1' || hostName.toLowerCase() === 'localhost';
+      if (method === 'GET' && (p0 === '/blank' || p0.startsWith('/blank?'))) {   // the page the panel starts on: nothing in it
+        respond(s, 200, 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title></title><body style="margin:0;background:#fff"></body>');
+        return;
+      }
+      if (!local) { respond(s, 403, 'text/plain', 'Host must be 127.0.0.1 or localhost.'); return; }
+      if ('origin' in h) { respond(s, 403, 'text/plain', "Rejected a connection from a web page (it sent an Origin). code_boss's panel browser takes DevTools clients only."); return; }
+      const p = p0.split('?')[0];
+      if (pbStr(h.upgrade).toLowerCase() === 'websocket') {
+        const page = p === '/devtools/page/' + this.targetId;
+        if (p !== this.browserPath && !page) { respond(s, 404, 'text/plain', 'No such DevTools target here: the browser is ' + this.browserPath + ', its one page /devtools/page/' + this.targetId + '.'); return; }
+        const key = h['sec-websocket-key'];
+        if (key == null) { respond(s, 400, 'text/plain', 'no Sec-WebSocket-Key'); return; }
+        const accept = createHash('sha1').update(key + GUID).digest('base64');
+        s.write('HTTP/1.1 101 Switching Protocols\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Accept: ' + accept + '\r\n\r\n');
+        s.setTimeout(0);
+        const c = new Client(this, new Ws(s, false, r.rest), page);
+        this.clients.add(c);
+        try { await c.run(); } finally { this.clients.delete(c); }
+        return;
+      }
+      if (p === '/json/version') {
+        let browser = 'Chrome', ua = '';
+        try { const real = objOf(JSON.parse(await httpGet(+(await this.chromeEndpoint())[0], '/json/version'))); browser = pbStr(real.Browser); ua = pbStr(real['User-Agent']); } catch { /* the defaults */ }
+        respond(s, 200, 'application/json', JSON.stringify({ Browser: browser, 'Protocol-Version': '1.3', 'User-Agent': ua, code_boss: "the right panel of code_boss's window (Settings > Agent's browser)", webSocketDebuggerUrl: 'ws://' + host + this.browserPath }));
+        return;
+      }
+      if (p === '/json' || p === '/json/list') { respond(s, 200, 'application/json', JSON.stringify([this.listed(host)])); return; }
+      if (p === '/json/new') {
+        let q = 'about:blank';
+        if (p0.includes('?')) { const raw = p0.slice(p0.indexOf('?') + 1).replace(/\+/g, ' '); try { q = decodeURIComponent(raw); } catch { q = raw; } }
+        await this.tellPage(this.siteOf(q === '' || q === 'about:blank' ? this.blankUrl() : q), true, false);
+        respond(s, 200, 'application/json', JSON.stringify(this.listed(host)));
+        return;
+      }
+      if (p === '/json/activate/' + this.targetId) { await this.tellPage(null, true, false); respond(s, 200, 'text/plain', 'Target activated'); return; }
+      if (p === '/json/close/' + this.targetId) { await this.tellPage(this.blankUrl(), false, false); respond(s, 200, 'text/plain', 'Target is closing'); return; }
+      if (p === '/json/protocol') { respond(s, 200, 'application/json', await httpGet(+(await this.chromeEndpoint())[0], '/json/protocol')); return; }
+      respond(s, 404, 'text/plain', 'Not here: /json/version, /json/list, /json/new, and the DevTools WebSocket.');
+    } catch {
+      try { s.destroy(); } catch { /* gone */ }
+    }
+  }
+
+  listed(host) {
+    return { description: '', devtoolsFrontendUrl: '', id: this.targetId, title: this.title, type: 'page', url: this.shown(this.url), webSocketDebuggerUrl: 'ws://' + host + '/devtools/page/' + this.targetId };
+  }
+
+  info(attached) {
+    return { targetId: this.targetId, type: 'page', title: this.title, url: this.shown(this.url), attached: !!attached, canAccessOpener: false, browserContextId: 'CB-PANEL' };
+  }
+}
+
+// ── one plugin's connection: a connection of its own to the window's Chrome, and the panel's page as its one target ──
+
+const err = (code, message) => ({ code, message });
+const gone = () => ({ error: err(-32000, "code_boss's window closed the connection") });
+const result = (a) => objOf(a.result);
+const errOf = (a) => (a.error == null ? null : pbStr(objOf(a.error).message));
+const attachParams = (targetId) => ({ targetId, flatten: true });
+const fetchPatterns = () => ({ patterns: [{ urlPattern: '*', resourceType: 'Document', requestStage: 'Response' }] });
+
+class Client {
+  constructor(br, ws, pageMode) {
+    this.br = br;
+    this.ws = ws;                // the plugin's
+    this.pageMode = pageMode;    // connected to the page's own address: every message is the page's, with no sessionId
+    this.up = null;              // to Chrome
+    this.seq = 1;
+    this.pending = new Map();
+    this.sid = hex(16);          // the page's session, as the plugin knows it - the same however often the iframe is attached again
+    this.topSid = null; this.topFrameId = null; this.adminSid = null; this.realSid = null; this.frameId = null;
+    this.attached = false; this.closed = false;
+    // BROWSER-LEVEL AUTO-ATTACH (puppeteer finds its pages this way: Target.setAutoAttach on the browser, then the
+    // attachedToTarget it is sent for each page): on, the panel's page is attached and announced - once per attach
+    this.autoAttach = false; this.announced = false;
+    this.sticky = [];
+    this.attaching = Promise.resolve();   // one attach at a time (the Java's attachLock)
+    // FRAMES INSIDE THE PAGE that are on another site (an app that loads another app): each is a target of its own in
+    // any Chrome. A plugin reaches them as it would anywhere - Target.setAutoAttach on the page's session - and their
+    // sessions are passed through as they are. Only targets INSIDE the panel's page ever get here.
+    this.children = new Set();
+  }
+
+  close() {
+    if (this.closed) return;
+    this.closed = true;
+    this.ws.close();
+    if (this.up) this.up.close();
+    const ds = [...this.pending.values()];
+    this.pending.clear();
+    for (const d of ds) d(gone());
+  }
+
+  async run() {
+    try {
+      const ep = await this.br.chromeEndpoint();
+      if (this.closed) return;
+      this.up = await Ws.connect(+ep[0], ep[1]);
+      if (this.closed) { this.up.close(); return; }
+      this.up.listen((text) => this.upstream(text), () => this.close());
+      this.call('Target.setDiscoverTargets', { discover: true }, null, null);   // the page's url and title as they change (targetInfoChanged)
+      // its downloads land where a plugin reads them, as with a Chrome of its own
+      if (this.br.downloadsDir) this.call('Browser.setDownloadBehavior', { behavior: 'allow', downloadPath: this.br.downloadsDir }, null, null);
+      await new Promise((ended) => this.ws.listen((text) => {
+        let m;
+        try { m = objOf(JSON.parse(text)); } catch { return; }
+        // a call that waits (a page to load, the iframe to be attached) does not hold up the next
+        this.handle(m).catch((e) => this.reply(m.id, null, err(-32000, "code_boss's panel browser: " + (e?.message || e)), 'sessionId' in m));
+      }, ended));
+    } catch (e) {
+      // Chrome's port is gone (the window closed or reopened): the plugin's connection ends with it
+      this.br.log('panel broker: ' + (e?.message || e));
+    } finally { this.close(); }
+  }
+
+  // ── to and from Chrome ──
+
+  call(method, params, session, done) {
+    const id = this.seq++;
+    const m = { id, method, params: params == null ? {} : params };
+    if (session != null) m.sessionId = session;
+    if (done) this.pending.set(id, done);
+    if (!this.up || !this.up.send(JSON.stringify(m))) { const d = this.pending.get(id); this.pending.delete(id); if (d) d(gone()); }
+    return id;
+  }
+
+  callSync(method, params, session, ms) {
+    return new Promise((resolve) => {
+      let settled = false, t = null;
+      const id = this.call(method, params, session, (a) => { if (settled) return; settled = true; if (t) clearTimeout(t); resolve(objOf(a)); });
+      if (!settled) t = setTimeout(() => { if (settled) return; settled = true; this.pending.delete(id); resolve({ error: err(-32000, method + ' had no answer in ' + Math.floor(ms / 1000) + ' s') }); }, ms);
+    });
+  }
+
+  upstream(text) {
+    let m;
+    try { m = objOf(JSON.parse(text)); } catch { return; }
+    if (m.id != null) { const d = this.pending.get(m.id); this.pending.delete(m.id); if (d) d(m); return; }
+    const method = pbStr(m.method);
+    const s = 'sessionId' in m ? pbStr(m.sessionId) : '';
+    const p = objOf(m.params);
+    if (s !== '' && (s === this.realSid || this.children.has(s))) {   // the page's own events (and its inner frames'), as they are
+      if (method === 'Target.attachedToTarget') this.children.add(pbStr(p.sessionId));
+      if (method === 'Target.detachedFromTarget') this.children.delete(pbStr(p.sessionId));
+      if (s === this.realSid) { this.topFrame(method, p); this.event(method, p); }
+      else this.passThrough(m);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (s === '') {
+      if (method === 'Target.detachedFromTarget') {
+        const g = pbStr(p.sessionId);
+        if (g === this.realSid) { this.realSid = null; this.attached = false; this.reattach().catch(() => {}); }
+        if (g === this.adminSid) this.adminSid = null;
+        if (g === this.topSid) this.topSid = null;
+      } else if (method === 'Target.targetInfoChanged') {
+        const ti = objOf(p.targetInfo);
+        if (this.frameId != null && this.frameId === pbStr(ti.targetId)) { this.br.url = pbStr(ti.url); this.br.title = pbStr(ti.title); }
+      }
+      return;
+    }
+    if (method === 'Fetch.requestPaused' && (s === this.adminSid || s === this.topSid)) { this.framable(s, p).catch(() => {}); return; }
+    if (method === 'Page.javascriptDialogOpening' && s === this.topSid && !this.br.windowsOwn(pbStr(p.url)) && this.attached) { this.event(method, p); return; }   // a dialog in the iframe is announced on the window's session
+    if (method === 'Page.javascriptDialogClosed' && s === this.topSid && this.attached) { this.event(method, p); return; }
+    if (method === 'Page.frameNavigated' && s === this.adminSid) {
+      const f = objOf(p.frame);
+      if (this.frameId != null && this.frameId === pbStr(f.id)) { const u = pbStr(f.url); this.br.url = u; this.br.tellPage(u, false, true); }
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** A document that refuses to be framed is answered without the two headers that refuse it; anything else goes on as it is. */
+  async framable(s, p) {
+    const rid = pbStr(p.requestId);
+    const hs = 'responseHeaders' in p ? arr(p.responseHeaders) : null;
+    const own = s === this.topSid && this.topFrameId != null && this.topFrameId === pbStr(p.frameId);
+    let refuses = false;
+    const kept = [];
+    if (hs != null && !own) {
+      for (const o of hs) {
+        const hh = objOf(o);
+        const n = pbStr(hh.name).toLowerCase();
+        if (n === 'x-frame-options') { refuses = true; continue; }
+        if (n === 'content-security-policy' && pbStr(hh.value).toLowerCase().includes('frame-ancestors')) {
+          refuses = true;
+          const v = pbStr(hh.value).split(';').filter((d) => !d.trim().toLowerCase().startsWith('frame-ancestors')).join(';');
+          if (v.trim() === '') continue;
+          kept.push({ name: hh.name, value: v });
+          continue;
+        }
+        kept.push(hh);
+      }
+    }
+    if (refuses) {
+      const b = await this.callSync('Fetch.getResponseBody', { requestId: rid }, s, 20000);
+      if (errOf(b) == null) {
+        const r = result(b);
+        let body = pbStr(r.body);
+        if (r.base64Encoded !== true) body = Buffer.from(body, 'utf8').toString('base64');
+        const a = await this.callSync('Fetch.fulfillRequest', { requestId: rid, responseCode: int(p.responseStatusCode), responseHeaders: kept, body }, s, 20000);
+        if (errOf(a) == null) return;
+      }
+    }
+    this.call('Fetch.continueResponse', { requestId: rid }, s, null);
+  }
+
+  // ── to the plugin ──
+
+  send(m) { if (!this.ws.send(JSON.stringify(m))) this.close(); }
+
+  reply(id, res, error, onSession) {
+    const m = { id: id === undefined ? null : id };
+    if (error != null) m.error = error; else m.result = res == null ? {} : res;
+    if (onSession && !this.pageMode) m.sessionId = this.sid;
+    this.send(m);
+  }
+
+  event(method, params) {
+    const m = { method, params };
+    if (!this.pageMode) m.sessionId = this.sid;
+    this.send(m);
+  }
+
+  browserEvent(method, params) {
+    if (this.pageMode) return;
+    this.send({ method, params });
+  }
+
+  async handle(m) {
+    const id = m.id;
+    const method = pbStr(m.method);
+    const p = objOf(m.params);
+    const hasSession = 'sessionId' in m;
+    const s = hasSession ? pbStr(m.sessionId) : (this.pageMode ? this.sid : '');
+    if (s === '') { await this.browserLevel(id, method, p); return; }
+    if (hasSession && this.children.has(s)) { this.innerLevel(id, method, p, s); return; }
+    if (s !== this.sid) { this.reply(id, null, err(-32001, 'Session with given id not found.'), hasSession); return; }
+    await this.pageLevel(id, method, p, hasSession);
+  }
+
+  async browserLevel(id, method, p) {
+    const t = pbStr(p.targetId);
+    const ours = t === '' || t === this.br.targetId;
+    if (method === 'Target.getTargets') { this.reply(id, { targetInfos: [this.br.info(this.attached)] }, null, false); return; }
+    if (method === 'Target.getTargetInfo') { if (!ours) { this.reply(id, null, err(-32602, 'No target with given id found'), false); return; } this.reply(id, { targetInfo: this.br.info(this.attached) }, null, false); return; }
+    if (method === 'Target.setDiscoverTargets') { if (p.discover === true) this.browserEvent('Target.targetCreated', { targetInfo: this.br.info(this.attached) }); this.reply(id, null, null, false); return; }
+    if (method === 'Target.setAutoAttach') {
+      this.autoAttach = p.autoAttach === true;
+      if (this.autoAttach) await this.announce();
+      this.reply(id, null, null, false);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (method === 'Target.setRemoteLocations') { this.reply(id, null, null, false); return; }
+    if (method === 'Target.getBrowserContexts') { this.reply(id, { browserContextIds: [] }, null, false); return; }
+    if (method === 'Target.createTarget') {
+      const u = pbStr(p.url).trim();
+      await this.navigate(u === '' ? 'about:blank' : u);
+      if (this.autoAttach) await this.announce();   // (a client that auto-attaches waits for the page's attachedToTarget)
+      this.reply(id, { targetId: this.br.targetId }, null, false);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (method === 'Target.attachToTarget') {
+      if (!ours) { this.reply(id, null, err(-32602, "No target with given id found (code_boss's panel browser has one page: " + this.br.targetId + ')'), false); return; }
+      const why = await this.attach();
+      if (why != null) { this.reply(id, null, err(-32000, why), false); return; }
+      this.browserEvent('Target.attachedToTarget', { sessionId: this.sid, targetInfo: this.br.info(true), waitingForDebugger: false });
+      this.announced = true;
+      this.reply(id, { sessionId: this.sid }, null, false);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (method === 'Target.detachFromTarget') { this.detach(); this.browserEvent('Target.detachedFromTarget', { sessionId: this.sid, targetId: this.br.targetId }); this.reply(id, null, null, false); return; }
+    if (method === 'Target.closeTarget') { if (!ours) { this.reply(id, null, err(-32602, 'No target with given id found'), false); return; } await this.closePage(); this.reply(id, { success: true }, null, false); return; }
+    if (method === 'Target.activateTarget') { await this.br.tellPage(null, true, false); this.reply(id, null, null, false); return; }
+    if (method === 'Browser.getVersion') { this.call(method, p, null, this.relay(id, false)); return; }
+    this.reply(id, null, err(-32601, "'" + method + "' is not available in code_boss's panel browser: it has one page (the right panel of code_boss's window). Use Target.getTargets / createTarget / attachToTarget / closeTarget, then everything else on that page's session."), false);
+  }
+
+  async pageLevel(id, method, p, onSession) {
+    const dot = method.indexOf('.');
+    const domain = dot > 0 ? method.slice(0, dot) : method;
+    if (method === 'Target.setDiscoverTargets') { this.reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+    if (method === 'Target.setAutoAttach') {   // the frames inside the page that are targets of their own
+      if (!this.attached) { const why = await this.attach(); if (why != null) { this.reply(id, null, err(-32000, why), onSession); return; } }
+      p.flatten = true;
+      this.remember(method, p);
+      this.call(method, p, this.realSid, this.relay(id, onSession));
+      return;
+    }
+    if (DENY.has(domain)) { this.reply(id, null, err(-32601, "'" + method + "' is not available on code_boss's panel page: it reaches past the page (the browser, its other tabs, or other sites' storage)."), onSession); return; }
+    if (method === 'Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride') { await this.size(int(p.width), int(p.height)); this.reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+    if (method === 'Emulation.clearDeviceMetricsOverride') { await this.size(0, 0); this.reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+    if (method === 'Page.bringToFront') { await this.br.tellPage(null, true, false); this.reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+    if (method === 'Page.close') { await this.closePage(); this.reply(id, null, null, onSession); return; }
+    if (!this.attached) { const why = await this.attach(); if (why != null) { this.reply(id, null, err(-32000, why), onSession); return; } }
+    if (method === 'Page.handleJavaScriptDialog') { this.call(method, p, this.topSid, this.relay(id, onSession)); return; }   // the dialog is the window's to answer (measured)
+    if (method === 'Page.navigate') {
+      p.url = this.br.siteOf(pbStr(p.url));
+      if ('frameId' in p && this.frameId != null && pbStr(p.frameId) === this.br.targetId) p.frameId = this.frameId;
+    }
+    this.remember(method, p);
+    const rs = this.realSid;
+    if (rs == null) { this.reply(id, null, err(-32000, "code_boss's panel page is being attached again (its page changed process); ask again"), onSession); return; }
+    this.call(method, p, rs, method === 'Page.getFrameTree' || method === 'Page.getResourceTree' ? this.topTree(id, onSession) : this.relay(id, onSession));
+  }
+
+  /** A frame inside the page, on its own session: the same rules as the page's, and nothing of the window. */
+  innerLevel(id, method, p, s) {
+    const dot = method.indexOf('.');
+    const domain = dot > 0 ? method.slice(0, dot) : method;
+    if (method === 'Target.setAutoAttach') p.flatten = true;
+    else if (DENY.has(domain)) { this.send({ id: id === undefined ? null : id, error: err(-32601, "'" + method + "' is not available on a frame in code_boss's panel page."), sessionId: s }); return; }
+    if (method === 'Page.handleJavaScriptDialog') { this.call(method, p, this.topSid, this.relayAs(id, s)); return; }
+    if (method === 'Page.navigate') p.url = this.br.siteOf(pbStr(p.url));
+    this.call(method, p, s, this.relayAs(id, s));
+  }
+
+  relayAs(id, s) {
+    return (a) => {
+      const m = { id: id === undefined ? null : id };
+      if ('error' in a) m.error = a.error; else m.result = 'result' in a ? a.result : {};
+      m.sessionId = s;
+      this.send(m);
+    };
+  }
+
+  passThrough(m) { this.send(m); }
+
+  /**
+   * THE PANEL'S FRAME IS THE PAGE'S TOP FRAME, as a plugin sees it: Chrome gives an iframe's own target a parentId (the
+   * frame in code_boss's window it sits in - measured), and a plugin takes "no parentId" to mean the top frame (the
+   * prod plugin re-reads the page's address and re-installs its runtime on it). So it is left out.
+   */
+  topFrame(method, p) {
+    if (method !== 'Page.frameNavigated' && method !== 'Page.frameAttached') return;
+    const f = objOf(p.frame);
+    if (this.frameId != null && this.frameId === pbStr(f.id)) delete f.parentId;
+    if (method === 'Page.frameAttached' && this.frameId != null && this.frameId === pbStr(p.frameId)) delete p.parentFrameId;
+  }
+
+  topTree(id, onSession) {
+    const r = this.relay(id, onSession);
+    return (a) => {
+      const tree = objOf(objOf(a.result).frameTree);
+      delete objOf(tree.frame).parentId;
+      r(a);
+    };
+  }
+
+  relay(id, onSession) {
+    return (a) => {
+      const m = { id: id === undefined ? null : id };
+      if ('error' in a) m.error = a.error; else m.result = 'result' in a ? a.result : {};
+      if (onSession && !this.pageMode) m.sessionId = this.sid;
+      this.send(m);
+    };
+  }
+
+  remember(method, p) {
+    const enable = method.endsWith('.enable'), disable = method.endsWith('.disable');
+    if (!enable && !disable && !STICKY.has(method)) return;
+    const domain = method.slice(0, method.indexOf('.'));
+    if (disable) { this.sticky = this.sticky.filter(([m]) => m !== domain + '.enable'); return; }
+    if (method !== 'Page.addScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument') this.sticky = this.sticky.filter(([m]) => m !== method);
+    this.sticky.push([method, { ...p }]);
+  }
+
+  // ── the panel's iframe ──
+
+  /** The iframe's frame id, read from code_boss's page (it is also the id of the iframe's own target, when it has one). */
+  async findFrame() {
+    if (this.topSid == null) {
+      const ts = arr(result(await this.callSync('Target.getTargets', null, null, 5000)).targetInfos);
+      let top = null;
+      for (const o of ts) { const ti = objOf(o); if (ti.type === 'page' && this.br.windowsOwn(pbStr(ti.url))) { top = pbStr(ti.targetId); break; } }
+      if (top == null) return null;
+      const a = await this.callSync('Target.attachToTarget', attachParams(top), null, 5000);
+      if (errOf(a) != null) return null;
+      this.topSid = pbStr(result(a).sessionId);
+      this.topFrameId = top;
+      await this.callSync('Page.enable', null, this.topSid, 5000);   // dialogs in the iframe are announced on the window's session
+      await this.callSync('Fetch.enable', fetchPatterns(), this.topSid, 5000);   // the iframe's first document (before it has a target of its own)
+    }
+    const e = await this.callSync('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: "document.getElementById('agentFrame')" }, this.topSid, 5000);
+    const oid = pbStr(objOf(result(e).result).objectId);
+    if (oid === '') return null;
+    const d = await this.callSync('DOM.describeNode', { objectId: oid }, this.topSid, 5000);
+    const f = pbStr(objOf(result(d).node).frameId);
+    await this.callSync('Runtime.releaseObject', { objectId: oid }, this.topSid, 2000);
+    return f === '' ? null : f;
+  }
+
+  /** Attach the plugin's session to the iframe's own target (giving the iframe one first, if it is still blank). null, or why not. */
+  attach() {
+    const run = this.attaching.then(() => this.attachNow());
+    this.attaching = run.then(() => {}, () => {});
+    return run;
+  }
+
+  async attachNow() {
+    if (this.attached && this.realSid != null) return null;
+    const until = Date.now() + 20000;
+    let asked = false;
+    while (!this.closed && Date.now() < until) {
+      const f = await this.findFrame();
+      if (f == null) {
+        if (this.topSid == null) return "code_boss's window was not found among the pages of its Chrome. Is it open?";
+        return "code_boss's window has no panel browser in it. Is Settings > Agent's browser on?";
+      }
+      this.frameId = f;
+      const a = await this.callSync('Target.attachToTarget', attachParams(f), null, 5000);
+      if (errOf(a) != null) {
+        // no target of its own yet: the iframe is still on about:blank, on the window's site. Put it on the blank page out here.
+        if (!asked) { await this.br.tellPage(this.br.blankUrl(), false, false); asked = true; }
+        await pbSleep(250);
+        continue;
+      }
+      // the first session is this broker's own (address line, framing); the plugin's is the next. Once the broker has
+      // its own, the probe above already is the plugin's.
+      let probe = pbStr(result(a).sessionId);
+      if (this.adminSid == null) {
+        this.adminSid = probe;
+        await this.callSync('Page.enable', null, probe, 5000);
+        await this.callSync('Fetch.enable', fetchPatterns(), probe, 5000);
+        const b = await this.callSync('Target.attachToTarget', attachParams(f), null, 5000);
+        if (errOf(b) != null) return "code_boss's panel page could not be attached: " + errOf(b);
+        probe = pbStr(result(b).sessionId);
+      }
+      this.realSid = probe;
+      this.attached = true;
+      const again = this.sticky.slice();
+      for (const [m, p] of again) await this.callSync(m, objOf(p), this.realSid, 5000);
+      return null;
+    }
+    return "code_boss's panel did not open a page of its own within 20 s.";
+  }
+
+  /** The iframe went to the window's own site (its target ended): put it back on 127.0.0.1, attach again, replay the session's settings. */
+  async reattach() {
+    if (this.closed) return;
+    const u = this.br.siteOf(this.br.url);
+    await this.br.tellPage(u === '' || u === 'about:blank' ? this.br.blankUrl() : u, false, false);
+    await pbSleep(300);
+    await this.attach();
+  }
+
+  /** The page attached, and said so to a client that auto-attaches - once per attach. */
+  async announce() {
+    if (this.announced) return;
+    if ((await this.attach()) != null) return;
+    this.announced = true;
+    this.browserEvent('Target.attachedToTarget', { sessionId: this.sid, targetInfo: this.br.info(true), waitingForDebugger: false });
+  }
+
+  detach() {
+    this.announced = false;
+    const r = this.realSid;
+    this.realSid = null;
+    this.attached = false;
+    this.sticky = [];
+    if (r != null) this.call('Target.detachFromTarget', { sessionId: r }, null, null);
+  }
+
+  async closePage() {
+    this.detach();
+    this.browserEvent('Target.detachedFromTarget', { sessionId: this.sid, targetId: this.br.targetId });
+    await this.br.tellPage(this.br.blankUrl(), false, false);
+  }
+
+  /** Target.createTarget: the panel goes to the address, and the call returns once the iframe has left the page it was on. */
+  async navigate(u) {
+    const to = u === 'about:blank' ? this.br.blankUrl() : this.br.siteOf(u);
+    const before = this.br.url;
+    await this.br.tellPage(to, true, false);
+    const until = Date.now() + 20000;
+    while (!this.closed && Date.now() < until) {
+      if (this.frameId == null) this.frameId = await this.findFrame();
+      if (this.frameId != null) {
+        const ts = arr(result(await this.callSync('Target.getTargets', null, null, 5000)).targetInfos);
+        for (const o of ts) {
+          const ti = objOf(o);
+          if (this.frameId !== pbStr(ti.targetId)) continue;
+          const now = pbStr(ti.url);
+          if (now === to || (now !== '' && now !== 'about:blank' && now !== before)) { this.br.url = now; this.br.title = pbStr(ti.title); return; }
+        }
+      }
+      await pbSleep(200);
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** The page's size as the plugin asked for it: the iframe is laid out at that size and scaled to fit the panel. 0,0: the panel's own. */
+  async size(w, h) {
+    if (this.topSid == null) await this.findFrame();
+    if (this.topSid == null) return;
+    this.call('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: 'window.panelBrowserSize && panelBrowserSize(' + Math.max(0, w) + ',' + Math.max(0, h) + ')' }, this.topSid, null);
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * The panel of one code_boss window as a browser for a plugin. Nothing listens until start() (or open(), which starts
+ * it when it is not running). tellPage(url, show, at) is how the page hears what the panel shows - code_boss's own
+ * /api/panel-browser, the same body the Java POSTs: url null keeps the page's, at: true says the page is there already.
+ */
+export function createPanelBroker({ profileDir, windowOrigin, tellPage, downloadsDir, log = () => {} }) {
+  const br = new Broker({ profileDir, windowOrigin, tellPage, downloadsDir, log });
+  let starting = null;
+  return {
+    start: () => br.start(),
+    stop: () => br.stop(),
+    alive: () => br.alive(),
+    get port() { return br.port; },
+    get browserPath() { return br.browserPath; },
+    get targetId() { return br.targetId; },
+    get id() { return br.id; },
+    blankUrl: () => br.blankUrl(),
+    brief: () => br.brief(),
+    /** The panel as a browser for a plugin: started (or reused), on the address it asked for. */
+    async open({ url, label } = {}) {
+      let u = url == null ? '' : String(url).trim();
+      if (u === '') u = 'about:blank';
+      if (u.length > 4000 || !(u.startsWith('http://') || u.startsWith('https://') || u === 'about:blank')) {
+        return no("A browser is opened on an http:// or https:// address, or about:blank - not on '" + (u.length > 80 ? u.slice(0, 80) + '...' : u) + "'.");
+      }
+      const reused = br.alive();
+      if (!reused) {
+        try { await (starting ||= br.start().finally(() => { starting = null; })); }
+        catch (e) { return no("The panel's browser could not be started: " + (e?.message || e)); }
+      }
+      br.label = label == null || String(label).trim() === '' ? 'default' : String(label).trim();
+      if (!reused || u !== 'about:blank') await br.tellPage(br.siteOf(u === 'about:blank' ? br.blankUrl() : u), true, false);
+      return { ok: true, reused, ...br.brief() };
+    },
+    /** Close: the panel is blanked (the broker stays, so the plugin's cached address keeps working). */
+    async close() {
+      await br.tellPage(br.blankUrl(), false, false);
+      return { ok: true, closed: true, id: br.id };
+    },
+  };
+}

+// A FILE'S HASH, READ AGAIN ONLY WHEN IT CHANGED (prod, 2026-11-13: the check below runs before every plugin reconcile -
+// every 5 s - and read and hashed every shipped file, piishape's 730 KB among them, synchronously, on a slow drive;

+const _fileSha = new Map();
+const fileSha = (p) => {
+  const st = statSync(p);
+  const key = `${st.mtimeMs}|${st.ctimeMs}|${st.size}`;

+const SYNC_OPS = {
+  mkdir: (d) => mkdirSync(d, { recursive: true }),
+  read: (f) => readFileSync(f, 'utf8'),
+  write: (f, t) => writeFileSync(f, t, 'utf8'),
+  readdir: (d) => readdirSync(d),
+  exists: (f) => existsSync(f),
+  rename: (a, b) => renameSync(a, b),
+  unlink: (f) => unlinkSync(f),
+  sha: (f) => fileSha(f),
+};
+const ASYNC_OPS = {
+  mkdir: (d) => mkdir(d, { recursive: true }),
+  read: (f) => readFile(f, 'utf8'),
+  write: (f, t) => writeFile(f, t, 'utf8'),
+  readdir: (d) => readdir(d),
+  exists: (f) => stat(f).then(() => true, () => false),
+  rename: (a, b) => rename(a, b),
+  unlink: (f) => unlink(f),
+  sha: (f) => fileShaAsync(f),
+};
+export function installBundledPlugins(dir, { sources = bundledPluginSources(), build = thisBuild() } = {}) {
+  const it = installSteps(dir, { sources, build });
+  let step = it.next();
+  while (!step.done) {
+    const [op, ...args] = step.value;
+    let v, err = null;
+    try { v = SYNC_OPS[op](...args); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    step = err ? it.throw(err) : it.next(v);
+  }
+  return step.value;
+}
+/** The same, without holding the server: what every reconcile runs (index.mjs beforeReconcile). */
+export async function installBundledPluginsAsync(dir, { sources = bundledPluginSources(), build = thisBuild() } = {}) {
+  const it = installSteps(dir, { sources, build });
+  let step = it.next();
+  while (!step.done) {
+    const [op, ...args] = step.value;
+    let v, err = null;
+    try { v = await ASYNC_OPS[op](...args); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    step = err ? it.throw(err) : it.next(v);
+  }
+  return step.value;
+}

=== add test/_panel-broker-node-harness.mjs
new 1f2b7185c169e3fb33fafec3
+/**
+ * THE NODE PANEL BROKER, RUN THE WAY THE JAVA HARNESS RUNS PanelBroker (test-panel-broker-e2e and
+ * test-panel-browser-hard-e2e with CB_PANEL_BROKER_IMPL=node): code_boss's window as App.openWindow opens it with the
+ * setting on (here headless, on a plain URL - --app needs a desktop), on its own profile and its own site
+ * (codeboss.localhost), and ONE broker for it. Prints `BROKER <port> <browserPath> <targetId>`; "reopen" on stdin closes
+ * the window and opens it again (a new Chrome port: REOPENED), and the end of stdin stops it all.
+ * Args (the Java harness's): <profileBase> <work> <instance id> <chrome.exe>
+ */
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { mkdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import http from 'node:http';
+import readline from 'node:readline';
+import { createPanelBroker } from '../src/agent/panel-broker.mjs';
+
+const [profileBase, work, idArg, exe] = process.argv.slice(2);
+const ID = Number(idArg), PORT = 18988 + ID - 1;   // App.Inst(ID).port
+const WINDOW_HOST = 'codeboss.localhost';
+const prof = path.join(profileBase, 'chrome-container-' + PORT);   // App.windowProfile
+const downloads = path.join(work, 'home', 'downloads');           // Browser.downloadsOutside, the container's tree = work
+
+function open() {
+  mkdirSync(prof, { recursive: true });
+  try { rmSync(path.join(prof, 'DevToolsActivePort')); } catch { /* a new profile */ }
+  return spawn(exe, ['--headless=new', '--remote-debugging-port=0', '--user-data-dir=' + prof, '--no-first-run', '--window-size=1400,900', `http://${WINDOW_HOST}:${PORT}/`], { stdio: 'ignore' });
+}
+
+// what the Java's tellPage does: POST the panel's address to code_boss's /api/panel-browser (2 s to connect, 5 s to answer)
+function tellPage(url, show, at) {
+  const body = {};
+  if (url != null) body.url = url;
+  body.show = !!show;
+  if (at) body.at = true;
+  const data = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body));
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    const req = http.request({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: PORT, path: '/api/panel-browser', method: 'POST', agent: false, timeout: 5000, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'content-length': data.length } }, (res) => { res.resume(); res.on('end', resolve); res.on('error', resolve); });
+    req.on('timeout', () => req.destroy());
+    req.on('error', resolve);
+    req.end(data);
+  });
+}
+
+let win = open();
+const broker = createPanelBroker({ profileDir: prof, windowOrigin: `http://${WINDOW_HOST}:${PORT}`, tellPage, downloadsDir: downloads, log: (m) => process.stderr.write(m + '\n') });
+await broker.start();
+process.stdout.write(`BROKER ${broker.port} ${broker.browserPath} ${broker.targetId}\n`);
+
+const killWin = () => { try { win.kill(); } catch { /* gone */ } };
+process.on('exit', killWin);
+const rl = readline.createInterface({ input: process.stdin });
+rl.on('line', async (l) => {
+  if (l.trim() !== 'reopen') return;
+  const was = win;
+  await new Promise((r) => { if (was.exitCode != null || was.signalCode != null) r(); else { was.once('exit', r); was.kill(); } });
+  win = open();
+  process.stdout.write('REOPENED\n');
+});
+rl.on('close', () => { broker.stop(); killWin(); process.exit(0); });
+

=== add test/test-bg-banner.mjs
new e31dc645d70baea90434b68d
+/**
+ * WHAT A STEP THAT STARTED A BACKGROUND PROCESS SAYS (the dev agent's report, prod 2026-11-13: a snippet started bg-13
+ * and stopped it with bgKill before it returned, and was told "bg-13 (pid null) ... is STILL RUNNING. Use bg_status or
+ * kill_background" - neither a function a snippet has). Through the real worker:
+ *   [1] started and left running: still running, with its pid and the snippet's own names (bgStatus, bgKill)
+ *   [2] started and stopped in the same snippet: said to have ended - not "STILL RUNNING"
+ * Run: node test/test-bg-banner.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { runMinimalWorker } from '../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs';
+import { killBackground, bgStatus } from '../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bgbanner-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'a.txt'), 'x');
+async function run(code) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const replies = [`<remote-workspace><js>${code}</js></remote-workspace>`, 'Done.\n/--- you ab12 ---'];
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: replies[Math.min(i++, 1)] } }] }; } };
+  const ev = [];
+  await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 't', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'go' }], projectDir: dir, requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => ev.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {} });
+  return String((ev.find((e) => e.type === 'tool-result') || {}).content || '');
+}
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] started and left running');
+  const a = await run('const p = await node(["-e", "setTimeout(()=>{},20000)"], { background: true }); return p.id;');
+  ok(/\[BACKGROUND\] This step started a background process that is STILL RUNNING: bg-\d+ \(pid \d+\)\. Look at it with bgStatus\("bg-\d+"\)/.test(a) && /bgKill\("bg-\d+"\)/.test(a) && !/bg_status|kill_background|pid null/.test(a), `still running, its pid, the snippet's own names (${(a.match(/\[BACKGROUND\][^\n]*/) || [''])[0].slice(0, 200)})`);
+  console.log('\n[2] started and stopped in the same snippet');
+  const b = await run('const p = await node(["-e", "setTimeout(()=>{},20000)"], { background: true }); await bgKill(p.id); return "stopped " + p.id;');
+  ok(/\[BACKGROUND\] The background process it started has already ended: bg-\d+/.test(b) && !/STILL RUNNING/.test(b), `said to have ended, not "still running" (${(b.match(/\[BACKGROUND\][^\n]*/) || [''])[0].slice(0, 200)})`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { for (const p of (bgStatus({ key: dir }).processes || [])) if (p.alive) { try { await killBackground({ key: dir, id: p.id }); } catch {} } }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+

=== add test/test-clone-ends-with-snippet.mjs
new b5a5e414b4d03335887da71a
+/**
+ * A CLONE ENDS WITH ITS SNIPPET (prod, 2026-11-13: "clone(...) timed out and was terminated by the JS eval timeout, the
+ * ... JGit pack stream kept an open file handle on _tmp_clone/.git/objects/incoming_*.pack, causing every subsequent
+ * remove("_tmp_clone") call to fail with EBUSY"). The clone ran on after its snippet ended - it was not in the snippet's
+ * own list of processes, which js_eval tree-kills when it ends. Through the real js_eval, against a server that takes the
+ * connection and never answers:
+ *   [1] the snippet's time budget ends it: the clone's git is killed with it - its connection closes, soon
+ *   [2] and the folder it was cloning into can be removed
+ * Run: node test/test-clone-ends-with-snippet.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync, existsSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { createServer } from 'node:net';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-clone-end-home-'));
+process.env.CB_JS_EVAL_BUDGET_MS = '4000';   // (read when js-eval.mjs loads: the snippet's whole budget)
+const { execute, extractContent } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// a "git server" that takes the connection and says nothing: the clone waits on it until something kills it
+const conns = [];
+const srv = createServer((s) => { const c = { open: true, closedAt: 0 }; conns.push(c); s.on('close', () => { c.open = false; c.closedAt = Date.now(); }); s.on('error', () => {}); });
+await new Promise((r) => srv.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
+const port = srv.address().port;
+const proj = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-clone-end-'));
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] the snippet\'s time budget ends it, and the clone with it');
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const out = await execute('js_eval', { code: `await clone("http://127.0.0.1:${port}/team/slow.git", { dir: "_tmp_clone" }); return "cloned";` }, {}, { cwd: proj, projectDir: proj, onLog: () => {} }).then(extractContent);
+  const ended = Date.now();
+  ok(conns.length >= 1, `git connected to the server (${conns.length})`);
+  ok(!/cloned/.test(String(out)), `the snippet did not finish the clone - its budget ended it (${String(out).slice(0, 100)})`);
+  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && conns.some((c) => c.open); i++) await sleep(250);
+  ok(conns.length && conns.every((c) => !c.open), `the clone's git was killed with the snippet: its connection closed (${conns.map((c) => c.open ? 'open' : (c.closedAt - ended) + 'ms after').join(', ')}; the snippet took ${ended - t0}ms)`);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the folder it was cloning into can be removed');
+  const dir = path.join(proj, '_tmp_clone');
+  let removed = !existsSync(dir), why = '';
+  for (let i = 0; i < 20 && !removed; i++) { try { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); removed = !existsSync(dir); } catch (e) { why = e.code || e.message; await sleep(250); } }
+  ok(removed, `removed (or never made) - no file of it held open${why ? ' (' + why + ')' : ''}`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL threw: ' + (e && e.stack || e)); }
+finally { srv.close(); }
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+

=== add test/test-pii-compare.mjs
new e20b331add36bf2b67094ec1
+/**
+ * COMPARISONS ON THE REAL VALUES, THE CODE GIVEN ONLY THE ANSWER (the developer, 2026-11-14: "have the tools return tokens,
+ * but just give the agent tools to make equals and comparisons that convert to tokens before doing the operation" - then
+ * "I meant includes(token, "888") would convert the token to the real value before doing the compare"; no cap: "Just
+ * making it a little harder to get large amounts of pii to the llm"). Through the real worker, with the shipped piishape:
+ *   [1] the snippet holds tokens again (CB_PII_AT_RETURN on by default): a literal typed in it does not match
+ *   [2] piiIncludes / piiEquals: the answer from the real value - a part of it too
+ *   [3] piiCompare: numbers as numbers, text as text
+ *   [4] piiReplace: the real text changed, handed back masked - the change made, no value in the clear
+ *   [5] nothing real reached the model through any of it
+ * Run: node test/test-pii-compare.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-piicmp-home-'));
+delete process.env.CB_PII_AT_RETURN;   // (the default: tokens)
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+const { createFilterPipeline } = await import('../src/plugins/filters.mjs');
+const piishape = (await import('../examples/plugins/piishape__1.mjs')).default;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+const block = (js) => `<remote-workspace><js>${js}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+const NAME = 'Rutherford Havisham', SSN = '482-11-9037';
+// (the values in the files - not the agent's own guesses, "9037" or "48213": what it typed is in the record of its calls,
+// piiIncludes(token, "9037") -> true, and it had them already)
+const REAL = [NAME, 'Rutherford', 'Havisham', SSN];
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-piicmp-'));
+await mkdir(path.join(dir, 'conf'), { recursive: true });
+await mkdir(path.join(dir, 'data'), { recursive: true });
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'conf', 'app.properties'), `support.contact_name=${NAME}\nsupport.ssn=${SSN}\nport=8080\n`);
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'data', 'person.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: NAME, ssn: SSN, visits: 48213 }));
+
+const filterOf = () => createFilterPipeline(() => piishape.filters.map((f) => ({ ...f, plugin: 'piishape' })));
+async function run(snippets) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const replies = [...snippets.map(block), 'Done.\n' + C];
+  const sent = [];
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) { sent.push(JSON.stringify(req.messages)); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)] } }] }; } };
+  const events = [];
+  await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 't', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'go' }], projectDir: dir,
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {}, contentFilter: filterOf() });
+  return { results: events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval'), sent: sent.join('\n') + '\n' + JSON.stringify(events) };
+}
+const leaks = (text) => REAL.filter((v) => String(text).includes(v));
+const val = (r) => { const s = String((r && r.content) || ''); const i = s.indexOf('\n\nWhat the snippet did, in order:\n'); return i < 0 ? s : s.slice(0, i); };
+// (the agent's own literals are written in pieces, so the model's copy of the snippet holds no whole value either)
+const N1 = '"Ruth" + "erford"', S4 = '"90" + "37"';
+
+const all = await run([`const t = await read("conf/app.properties"); const j = await readJson("data/person.json");
+  const name = t.split("\\n")[0].split("=")[1];
+  return {
+    literal: t.includes(${N1}),
+    includes: await piiIncludes(t, ${N1}), part: await piiIncludes(name, ${S4}) || await piiIncludes(t, ${S4}), not: await piiIncludes(t, "Smithers"),
+    equals: await piiEquals(name, ${N1} + " Hav" + "isham"), equalsNot: await piiEquals(name, "Someone Else"), equalsNum: await piiEquals(j.visits, "48" + "213"),
+    more: await piiCompare(j.visits, 1000), less: await piiCompare(j.visits, 99999), same: await piiCompare(j.visits, 48 * 1000 + 213), text: await piiCompare(name, "Aaron"),
+    replaced: await piiReplace(t, "port=8080", "port=9090"),
+    typed: typeof j.visits,
+  };`]);
+const r = all.results[0];
+const v = val(r).replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
+
+console.log('\n[1] the snippet holds tokens');
+ok(/"literal": *false/.test(v), `a literal typed in the code does not match the read text: it holds a token there (${v.slice(0, 120)})`);
+
+console.log('\n[2] piiIncludes / piiEquals');
+ok(/"includes": *true/.test(v) && /"part": *true/.test(v) && /"not": *false/.test(v), 'piiIncludes answers from the real text - a part of a value too, and false when it is not there');
+ok(/"equals": *true/.test(v) && /"equalsNot": *false/.test(v) && /"equalsNum": *true/.test(v), 'piiEquals: the whole value, another value, a number token against its digits');
+
+console.log('\n[3] piiCompare');
+ok(/"more": *1/.test(v) && /"less": *-1/.test(v) && /"same": *0/.test(v) && /"text": *1/.test(v), 'numbers compared as numbers, text as text: -1, 0, 1');
+
+console.log('\n[4] piiReplace');
+ok(/port=9090/.test(v) && !/port=8080/.test(v) && /support\.contact_name=#/.test(v), `the real text changed, handed back masked: the change made, the name a token again (${(/"replaced": *"([^"]*)"/.exec(v) || [])[1]})`);
+
+console.log('\n[5] nothing real reached the model');
+ok(!leaks(all.sent).length, `none of the values, through any of it (${leaks(all.sent).join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-snippet-real-values.mjs
new bfd940ea55d34ebfd3e00cd7
+/**
+ * CB_PII_AT_RETURN=0: A SNIPPET SEES THE REAL VALUES - the default for one day (2026-11-13; the developer: "since our org
+ * actually allows pii to go through the api now, let's change so that js scripts see unmasked values"), and the switch
+ * since 2026-11-14, when tokens came back with piiIncludes & co (test-pii-compare.mjs). Through the real worker, with the
+ * shipped piishape:
+ *   [1] the code holds the real text: a literal it types matches, .replace finds it, a JSON number is a number, a CSV's rows
+ *   [2] what goes back to the model is masked: nothing from the file reaches it - split, re-cased, behind a prefix
+ *   [3] ...but what the code COMPUTED is shown (a count), and so are sentences code_boss wrote ("Call ...")
+ *   [5] a result over 2 MB goes to a file whole - it is not refused for being too big to mask
+ * Run: node test/test-snippet-real-values.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-realval-home-'));
+process.env.CB_PII_AT_RETURN = '0';   // (the mode this file is about - tokens are the default)
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+const { createFilterPipeline } = await import('../src/plugins/filters.mjs');
+const piishape = (await import('../examples/plugins/piishape__1.mjs')).default;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+const block = (js) => `<remote-workspace><js>${js}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+const NAME = 'Rutherford Havisham', SSN = '482-11-9037';
+const REAL = [NAME, 'Rutherford', 'Havisham', 'RUTHERFORD', 'HAVISHAM', SSN, '9037'];
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-realval-'));
+await mkdir(path.join(dir, 'conf'), { recursive: true });
+await mkdir(path.join(dir, 'data'), { recursive: true });
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'conf', 'app.properties'), `support.contact_name=${NAME}\nsupport.ssn=${SSN}\nport=8080\n`);
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'data', 'person.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: NAME, ssn: SSN, visits: 48213 }));
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'data', 'people.csv'), `name,ssn\n"${NAME}",${SSN}\n`);
+
+const filterOf = () => createFilterPipeline(() => piishape.filters.map((f) => ({ ...f, plugin: 'piishape' })));
+async function run(snippets) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const replies = [...snippets.map((s) => (typeof s === 'function' ? (shown) => block(s(shown)) : block(s))), 'Done.\n' + C];
+  const sent = [];
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) { const shown = JSON.stringify(req.messages); sent.push(shown); const r = replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)]; yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: typeof r === 'function' ? r(shown) : r } }] }; } };
+  const events = [];
+  await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 't', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'go' }], projectDir: dir,
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {}, contentFilter: filterOf() });
+  return { results: events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval'), sent: sent.join('\n') + '\n' + JSON.stringify(events) };
+}

+ * WHAT IS CODE_BOSS'S OWN WORDING IN A REQUEST, AND WHAT IS NOT (prod, 2026-11-13: the developer's Windows user name

+import { spawnBackground, killBackground } from '../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs';

+/**
+ * A RUN CODE_BOSS STARTED THAT COMES BACK EMPTY ENDS WITH NOTHING (src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs `wokenBy`; prod feedback,

+ *   [3] woken, but a message the developer typed arrives meanwhile: a run like theirs from there - the stand-in stays
+ *   [4] woken, a reply with text: shown as ever

+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';

+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-woken-'));
+const STAND_IN = /don't have a written reply/;
+
+const run = async (replies, { wokenBy = null, typed = null } = {}) => {
+  let i = 0;
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)] } }] }; } };

+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {},
+  });
+  const shown = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => e.content).join('\n');
+  return { result, events, shown, calls: i };
+};

+  ok(r.calls === 3, 'the empty-reply nudges still ran first (3 calls)');

+  const r = await run(['All todos are done; nothing left to carry on with.'], { wokenBy: 'callback' });
